Second transcription's note: Every STOP in Steps 1 to 5 and Worksheets A and B gives zero. The refusals cover cases the IRS sends to other forms or to Pub. 596: Form 8862 (an earlier EIC reduced or disallowed); Form 2555; nonresident aliens; Schedule E, personal property rental, Form 8814 and passive activities (Step 2, question 4); and Form 4797. The option of having the IRS figure the credit is not modelled.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can't take the credit." Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.json; 1,325 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 110 partial records; 1,935 comparisons): 1,830 of 1,935 agree; no value difference; 38 candidate-unknown (8 on completed returns), 55 candidate-refuses (all on refused returns), 9 project-unknown, 3 project-refuses. The candidate-unknown come from its eic.h_limit / eic.h_agi_ok, which choose the Step 1 limit by the count of qualifying children with valid SSNs, so they need each dependent's answers (dependent.us_half) even when line 11b is at or above the largest limit and question 1 is No whatever the count; the project tests that limit first (eic.below_top) and the interview does not ask those answers there. A candidate that needs answers the source does not need to determine the result is a model error (README), as in rounds 3 and 4. Witnesses: classm.f8995a.1 (head of household, line 11b $388,706.00) project $0.00, candidate unknown (dependent[dep_c].us_half); tcw.hoh.b1 (incomplete) likewise. The candidate-refuses are its declared gates (scope.other_line16 on classm.clcw26.3.deepseek and classm.eic.7.deepseek; dependent.married, i1040gi p. 44, Married child, on classm.f8880.4; dependent.other_claimant, p. 44, Qualifying child of more than one person, on classm.carloan.1), all on returns the project also refuses: scope. The 3 project-refuses are cited gates (i8889 HSA screen on stage16.cleanup.refuse.f8889.screen_no_amounts; Form 1099-R special exceptions; sa.dependent, i1040gi p. 42, Step 4, question 6, on stage9.eic.refuse.dependent_of_another): scope. The 9 project-unknown (stage14.ho.gross_limit, stage14.s179.income_limit, audit.sa.negative_agi_medical) withhold f1040.tax_exempt_interest, part of the Step 2 investment income the interview asks of every filer; the candidate reaches $0 only because its eligibility test fails first (representation). Round 2 of Stage 19: the only eic change since round 4 is BUG-0195, the wording of eic.us_home (i1040gi p. 42, Step 4, question 3: "Was your main home, and your spouse's if filing a joint return, in the United States for more than half of 2025?"), which the project label and question now quote; it changes no expression, and no difference here turns on it. Project stands.

Second transcription's note: Question 6 is also reached by unmarried filers. The box concerns the special rule for separated spouses, which applies only to a married filer not filing jointly, so it is checked only for a married filer filing separately or as head of household who passed questions 4 and 5.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 41, Step 3, question 3: "Are you a married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household? Yes. Continue No. Skip questions 4 and 5; go to question 6"; question 6: "No. Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses"; p. 44, Special rule for separated spouses. Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.json; 1,325 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 110 partial records; 1,935 comparisons): 1,891 of 1,935 agree; no value difference; 3 candidate-unknown (1 on a completed return), 32 candidate-refuses and 9 project-refuses, all on refused returns. The candidate restricts the box to a married filer filing separately or as head of household who passed questions 4 and 5 and answers No to question 6, which is the project's eic.separated_box term for term (eic.married_rules, eic.step3_married, eic.qc_of_another, after eic.reached_step3) and the chosen reading of amb.2025.separated_spouses_box. The unknowns are the child-count limit again. The candidate-unknown come from its eic.h_limit / eic.h_agi_ok, which choose the Step 1 limit by the count of qualifying children with valid SSNs, so they need each dependent's answers (dependent.us_half) even when line 11b is at or above the largest limit and question 1 is No whatever the count; the project tests that limit first (eic.below_top) and the interview does not ask those answers there. A candidate that needs answers the source does not need to determine the result is a model error (README), as in rounds 3 and 4. Witness: audit.household.hoh_married_no_community_property (completed) project no, candidate unknown (dependent[child].us_half). The refusals are cited gates on refused returns (dependent.married, dependent.other_claimant for the candidate; sa.excluded_income on classm.eic.6, scope.other_line16, scope.other_income for the project): scope. Round 2 of Stage 19: the only eic change since round 4 is BUG-0195, the wording of eic.us_home (i1040gi p. 42, Step 4, question 3: "Was your main home, and your spouse's if filing a joint return, in the United States for more than half of 2025?"), which the project label and question now quote; it changes no expression, and no difference here turns on it. Project stands.

The candidate looks up line 2 on every return. Shown after a STOP: classm.f4952.2.deepseek (completed) hidden, candidate $308.00; classm.f4952.2 likewise. The candidate-unknown are the child-count limit unknowns of eic.credit (classm.f8995a.1, dependent[dep_c].us_half). The 96 project-unknown are all on refused returns where eic.eligible, the line's shown_when, itself refuses (triage counts a line as shown when its shown_when itself refuses, T-070 (2)): classm.dependent.2.deepseek (eic.eligible refused on div.bad_components) has line 1 $239,545.00, above the table ("no band"), and the candidate $0.00; on a return where eic.eligible is true, Step 5, question 3 (eic.step5_income, earned income below the limit) keeps line 1 inside the table, so no reachable return shows it. The refusals on both sides are cited gates on refused returns (scope). No value difference. Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can't take the credit." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A: "Use this worksheet only if you answered “No” to Step 5, question 2. Otherwise, use Worksheet B." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A, line 6: "This is your earned income credit." Triage executable-triage-v2 (triage/eic.json; 1,325 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 110 partial records; 1,935 comparisons): 1,824 of 1,935 agree; 25 candidate-shows-skipped (10 on completed returns), 48 candidate-unknown (13 on completed returns), 37 candidate-refuses, 1 project-refuses; no value difference. The candidate figures line 6 on every return. Witnesses: stage13.eic_spouse_no_ssn (completed; Step 1, question 2 STOP) hidden, candidate $649.00; stage16.cleanup.mut.clwb_three_figured (completed) hidden, candidate $1,294.00. The candidate-unknown are the child-count limit unknowns (classm.f8995a.1). The refusals are cited gates on refused returns (scope). Round 2 of Stage 19: the only eic change since round 4 is BUG-0195, the wording of eic.us_home (i1040gi p. 42, Step 4, question 3: "Was your main home, and your spouse's if filing a joint return, in the United States for more than half of 2025?"), which the project label and question now quote; it changes no expression, and no difference here turns on it. Project stands.

## Refusal gates listed by the second transcription

Reported, not judged: the project often refuses before a worksheet is used, on the line that chooses it, which a per-line comparison cannot see.

| line | gates listed | not read by the project's line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| eic.credit | household.credit_disallowed, s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.other_line16, scope.schedule1, sd.other_forms | - |
| eic.check_27c | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | - |
| eic.clergy | scope.schedule1 | - |
| (Step 5, 1) | scope.schedule1 | - |
| (Step 5, 2) | s1.line8_other, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other, scope.schedule1 |
| (Step 5, 3) | s1.line8_other, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| (Step 5, 4) | s8812.combat_pay | - |
| (Step 5, 5) | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 4b | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 6 (eic.wsb_6) | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 1 | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 2 | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | s1.line8_other |
| 4 | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | - |
| 6 (eic.line6) | s1.line8_other, s8812.combat_pay, scope.schedule1 | - |

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: eic.step5_line1
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "The Clergy and Church employees instructions change what goes on this line. Clergy income and church employee income are refused by scope.schedule1, so this line copies line 1z only."

  - id: eic.step5_line2
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [s1.line8_other, scope.schedule1]
    note: "Excluded Medicaid waiver payments (Schedule 1 line 8s) are outside the tool's scope and are refused, so this line is zero when it is computed."

  - id: eic.step5_line3
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.step5_line1, take: eic.step5_line2}

  - id: eic.step5_line4
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay]
    note: "Nontaxable combat pay and the election to include it are refused through s8812.combat_pay. Without combat pay this line is zero."

  - id: eic.earned_income
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.step5_line3, eic.step5_line4]}

  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 5, question 2: Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [s1.has_sc, {const: true}]}
    note: "Self-employment in scope means a Schedule C business. Clergy, church employee income and statutory employees are refused by scope.schedule1, so only s1.has_sc is tested."

  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 27b: a minister, member of a religious order who has not taken a vow of poverty, or a Christian Science practitioner, filing Schedule SE whose line 2 includes an amount also reported on Form 1040 line 1z (Step 5, question 1: filing Schedule SE as clergy or with church employee income of $108.28 or more)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [scope.schedule1, {const: true}]}
    refuses: [scope.schedule1]
    note: "No input asks about clergy directly. Clergy and church employee income fall under the scope.schedule1 screen, which refuses this line. When it is answered no, the line is false."

  - id: eic.h_se_line3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, 1a: Schedule SE, Part I, line 3 (persons filing Schedule SE)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.se_line2, where: {op: test, eq: [person.files_se, {const: true}]}}
    note: "Farm income is refused (person.se_screen), so Schedule SE line 3 equals line 2. Line 1b (Schedule SE lines 4b and 5a: optional methods and church employee income) is refused by person.se_screen and is not entered."

  - id: eic.h_se_line13
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, 1d: Schedule SE, Part I, line 13"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.se_line13, where: {op: test, eq: [person.files_se, {const: true}]}}

  - id: eic.h_1e
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, 1e: Subtract line 1d from line 1c"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.h_se_line3, take: eic.h_se_line13}

  - id: eic.h_2c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Worksheet B, 2c: net profit or (loss) from Schedule C, line 31 (self-employed not required to file Schedule SE)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.sc_total, where: {op: test, eq: [person.files_se, {const: false}]}}
    note: "Farm profit (2a) and Schedule K-1 amounts are outside the tool's scope. Part 3 (statutory employee income) is refused by scope.schedule1 and is not entered."

  - id: eic.wsb_4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.h_1e, eic.h_2c, eic.earned_income]}

  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: eic.wsb_4b, take: {param: ss.zero}}
    note: "If line 4b is zero or less, the IRS says STOP; that stop is applied to eic.credit. The zero floor here only keeps the declared type."

  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}

  - id: eic.h_mfj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Filing status is married filing jointly"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}

  - id: eic.h_married_sep
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Are you a married taxpayer whose filing status is married filing separately or head of household?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [household.married, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_kids
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of qualifying children who have valid SSNs"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn, {const: true}]}
    note: "The tie-breaker rules for a child who is the qualifying child of more than one person, and the married-child rule, are screened for each dependent (dependent.other_claimant, dependent.divorced_rule, dependent.married) and are assumed to be reflected in dependent.eic_child."

  - id: eic.h_qc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Number of children who meet the conditions to be your qualifying child for the EIC (with or without a valid SSN)"
    type: Count
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: dependent, field: dependent.one, where: {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}}

  - id: eic.h_has_qc
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, question 1: Do you have at least one child who meets the conditions to be your qualifying child for the purpose of claiming the EIC?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}

  - id: eic.h_lim0_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, no children with valid SSNs ($19,104)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim0_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, no children with valid SSNs, married filing jointly ($26,214)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit0.joint}
  - id: eic.h_lim1_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 1 child with a valid SSN ($50,434)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim1_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 1 child with a valid SSN, married filing jointly ($57,554)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit1.joint}
  - id: eic.h_lim2_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 2 children with valid SSNs ($57,310)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim2_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 2 children with valid SSNs, married filing jointly ($64,430)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit2.joint}
  - id: eic.h_lim3_other
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 3 or more children with valid SSNs ($61,555)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.other}
  - id: eic.h_lim3_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Limit, 3 or more children with valid SSNs, married filing jointly ($68,675)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: eic.limit3.joint}

  - id: eic.h_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Income limit (Step 1, question 1; Step 5, question 3; Worksheet B, line 5) for your number of children with valid SSNs and filing status"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim0_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim0_joint
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim1_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim1_joint
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim2_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 2, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim2_joint
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim3_other
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 3, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}]}
          then: eic.h_lim3_joint

  - id: eic.h_agi_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 1: is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than the limit?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, eic.h_limit]}

  - id: eic.h_ssn_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 1, question 2: Do you, and your spouse if filing a joint return, have a social security number issued on or before the due date of your 2025 return (including extensions) that allows you to work and is valid for EIC purposes?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [household.all_eic_ssn, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_7a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2: Form 1040 line 7a (if line 7a is a loss, enter -0-)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: f1040.line7a, take: {param: ss.zero}}

  - id: eic.h_investment
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 1: Investment income (lines 2a + 2b + 3b + 7a)"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [f1040.line2a, f1040.line2b, f1040.line3b, eic.h_7a]}

  - id: eic.h_inv_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 2, question 2: Is your investment income $11,950 or less?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: [eic.h_investment, {param: eic.investment_limit}]}
    note: "If investment income is more than $11,950, question 3 asks about Form 4797. Form 4797 is outside the tool's scope (sd.other_forms, scope.schedule1), so the answer there is No: STOP, no credit."

  - id: eic.h_r3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Reached Step 3 (passed Step 1 and Step 2)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_agi_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_ok, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_inv_ok, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.h_step3_blocked
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3, questions 3 to 5: married filing separately or head of household, same principal residence for the last 6 months, and not legally separated (STOP)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_step3_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 3 passed (filer with a qualifying child)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step3_blocked, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_age_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, question 2: persons on the return born after December 31, 1960, and before January 2, 2001"
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_after_1960, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [person.born_before_2001, {const: true}]}
    note: "For a spouse or taxpayer who died in 2025 the IRS refers to Pub. 596, which is not in the text provided. The birth-date test is applied as printed."

  - id: eic.h_step4_pre
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4, questions 1 and 2 passed (no qualifying child; not married filing separately or head of household; age 25 to 64)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, ge: [eic.h_age_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}

  - id: eic.h_step4_ok
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Step 4 passed (filer without a qualifying child)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step4_pre, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}
            - op: test
              all:
                - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
                - {op: test, eq: [sa.dependent, {const: false}]}
    note: "Question 6 (can you be claimed as a dependent on someone else's return) is read from sa.dependent, whose wording includes the same exception for a person who files only to claim a refund."

  - id: eic.h_r5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Reached Step 5 (passed Steps 1 to 4)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_r3, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step3_ok, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step4_ok, {const: true}]}

  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box in the Dependents section that discusses the special rule for separated spouses (Step 3, question 6: No)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_r3, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_married_sep, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step3_blocked, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
    note: "Question 6 is also reached by unmarried filers. The box concerns the special rule for separated spouses, which applies only to a married filer not filing jointly, so it is checked only for a married filer filing separately or as head of household who passed questions 4 and 5."

  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5 (Worksheet B filers: total earned income from Part 4, line 4b, entered on line 6)"
    type: Dollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.earned_income
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.wsb_6
    note: "Worksheet B lines 6 to 11 repeat Worksheet A lines 1 to 6 word for word. Lines 1 to 6 here serve both: line 1 is Step 5 earned income (Worksheet A) or Worksheet B line 6."

  - id: eic.h_t0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, no qualifying children, amount on line 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_t1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, one qualifying child, amount on line 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_t2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, two qualifying children, amount on line 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_t3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, three or more qualifying children, amount on line 1"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, {param: eic.table_start}]}
              - {op: test, ge: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.line1, {param: eic.table_start}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_t0
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.line1, {param: eic.table_start}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_t1
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.line1, {param: eic.table_start}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 2, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_t2
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.line1, {param: eic.table_start}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}, {op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 3, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_t3
    note: "The table begins at $1. Amounts below $1, and amounts at or above the income limit (the table footnotes say you can't take the credit there), are entered as zero."

  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}

  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}

  - id: eic.h_below
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 question: is the amount on line 3 less than $10,620 ($17,730 if married filing jointly) with no qualifying children with valid SSNs, or less than $23,350 ($30,470 if married filing jointly) with 1 or more?"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold0.other}]}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}]}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold1.other}]}]}
        - {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: true}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}]}]}

  - id: eic.h_show5
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 5 is filled in (line 4 is No and the line 5 question is No)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_below, {const: false}]}

  - id: eic.h_a0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, no qualifying children, amount on line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_a1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, one qualifying child, amount on line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_a2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, two qualifying children, amount on line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_a3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "EIC Table, three or more qualifying children, amount on line 3"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}

  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    shown_when: eic.h_show5
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            any:
              - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.table_start}]}
              - {op: test, ge: [eic.line3, eic.h_limit]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.line3, {param: eic.table_start}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_limit]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 0, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_a0
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.line3, {param: eic.table_start}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_limit]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 1, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_a1
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.line3, {param: eic.table_start}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_limit]}, {op: test, eq: [eic.h_kids, {const: 2, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_a2
        - guard: {op: test, all: [{op: test, ge: [eic.line3, {param: eic.table_start}]}, {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, eic.h_limit]}, {op: test, ge: [eic.h_kids, {const: 3, type: Count}]}]}
          then: eic.h_a3
    note: "Line 5 is left blank unless h_show5 is true. Its value is computed either way and line 6 reads it only when h_show5 is true."

  - id: eic.h_min56
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Look at the amounts on lines 5 and 2. Then, enter the smaller amount on line 6."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}

  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6 (eic.line6)"
    label: "This is your earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_show5, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_min56
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_show5, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.line2

  - id: eic.h_claim
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "All Steps passed and no STOP in Step 5 or Worksheet B, Part 4"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_r5, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, lt: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [eic.wsb_4b, {const: 0}]}

  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Earned income credit (enter on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 27a)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_claim, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.line6
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_claim, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
    refuses: [household.credit_disallowed, sa.excluded_income, scope.other_income, scope.schedule1, scope.other_line16, sd.other_forms]
    note: "Every STOP in Steps 1 to 5 and Worksheets A and B gives zero. The refusals cover cases the IRS sends to other forms or to Pub. 596: Form 8862 (an earlier EIC reduced or disallowed); Form 2555; nonresident aliens; Schedule E, personal property rental, Form 8814 and passive activities (Step 2, question 4); and Form 4797. The option of having the IRS figure the credit is not modelled."

  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Check the box on line 27c if you do not want to claim the earned income credit or if you have been instructed to check the box in the instructions for line 27a"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_agi_ok, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_ssn_ok, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_r3, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_has_qc, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step3_blocked, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_r3, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step4_pre, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_r3, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_step4_pre, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_mfj, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_r5, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, le: [eic.wsb_4b, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_r5, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [eic.wsb_4b, {const: 0}]}
            - {op: test, ge: [eic.line1, eic.h_limit]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_claim, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.line2, {const: 0}]}
    note: "Covers every instruction to check line 27c: Step 1 question 2; Step 3 question 6; Step 4 questions 3 and 5; Worksheet B line 4b zero or less and line 5 No; Worksheet A line 2 or Worksheet B line 7 zero. The nonresident alien case is refused (scope.other_income). There is no input for choosing not to claim the credit, so that case is not represented."
```
```
